1	/**
2	 * `contextSize` domain (L4) — wire Model (`ContextSizeModel`) and the
3	 * `context_size.measured` (`contextSizeMeasured`) Op for the last measured
4	 * context token count.
5	 *
6	 * Declares the deterministic measured prefix as `{ length, tokens }` (initial
7	 * `{ 0, 0 }`): the length (in messages) and total token count of the most
8	 * recent `context_size.measured` record. That record is written from two live
9	 * paths: `llmRequester` after each measured exchange (a true LLM-reported
10	 * count), and `contextMemoryService` cascading alongside every context mutation
11	 * that changes the measured prefix (`clear` resets, `applyCompaction` adopts
12	 * `tokensAfter`, and `undo` rebases to an estimate when the aggregate is
13	 * truncated); `append` is intentionally not cascaded because new messages are
14	 * the unmeasured tail. The Op is live-only because `context_size.measured` is
15	 * not a v1 record type: resume starts from `{ 0, 0 }` and
16	 * `contextSizeService.get()` estimates until the next measured exchange.
17	 * `apply` is pure — it normalizes the payload and returns the SAME reference
18	 * on a no-op so the wire's reference-equality gate stays quiet — and carries no
19	 * non-determinism (the last measured record wins). The sparse
20	 * `measuredPrefixTokens` array and the per-message live `estimates` are
21	 * intentionally NOT in the Model. Consumed by the Agent-scope
22	 * `contextSizeService`.
23	 */
24	
25	import { defineModel } from '#/wire/model';
26	import { defineOp } from '#/wire/op';
27	
28	export interface ContextSizeState {
29	  readonly length: number;
30	  readonly tokens: number;
31	}
32	
33	export const ContextSizeModel = defineModel<ContextSizeState>('contextSize', () => ({
34	  length: 0,
35	  tokens: 0,
36	}));
37	
38	export interface ContextSizeMeasuredPayload {
39	  readonly length: number;
40	  readonly tokens: number;
41	}
42	
43	export const contextSizeMeasured = defineOp(ContextSizeModel, 'context_size.measured', {
44	  persist: false,
45	  apply: (s, p: ContextSizeMeasuredPayload): ContextSizeState => {
46	    const length = normalizeMeasuredLength(p.length);
47	    const tokens = Math.max(0, p.tokens);
48	    if (s.length === length && s.tokens === tokens) return s;
49	    return { length, tokens };
50	  },
51	  toEvent: (_p, state) => ({
52	    type: 'agent.status.updated' as const,
53	    contextTokens: state.tokens,
54	  }),
55	});
56	
57	function normalizeMeasuredLength(length: number): number {
58	  if (!Number.isFinite(length)) return 0;
59	  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(length));
60	}

1	/**
2	 * `spine` domain (L4) — the projection fold that turns the append-only
3	 * `contextMemory` history into the view sent to the model.
4	 *
5	 * `foldSpine` is a pure transform: it drops everything before the current root
6	 * epoch (keeping the epoch summary), replaces each outermost closed node's raw
7	 * message span with a single `<spine_memory>` user message (nested closed nodes
8	 * ride inside their nearest closed ancestor's span, so nothing is folded twice),
9	 * numbers surviving real user requests with `[U#]` anchors, and appends a
10	 * synthetic `<spine_status>` orientation line. The stored history is never
11	 * mutated; token numbers for the status line are precomputed by the `spine`
12	 * service and passed in. Consumed by `spineService.fold`.
13	 */
14	
15	import type { ContentPart } from '#/app/llmProtocol/message';
16	import type { ContextMessage } from '#/agent/contextMemory/types';
17	
18	import type { SpineNode, SpineState } from './spineOps';
19	import { parentNodeId } from './spineTree';
20	
21	export interface SpineFoldStatus {
22	  readonly cursorId: string;
23	  readonly summary: string;
24	  readonly parentId: string | null;
25	  readonly cursorContext: number;
26	  readonly contextLeft: number | undefined;
27	}
28	
29	export interface SpineFoldInput {
30	  readonly state: SpineState;
31	  readonly epochSummaryMessage?: ContextMessage;
32	  readonly status?: SpineFoldStatus;
33	}
34	
35	interface ClosedSpan {
36	  readonly node: SpineNode;
37	  readonly openedAt: number;
38	  readonly closedAt: number;
39	}
40	
41	export function foldSpine(
42	  messages: readonly ContextMessage[],
43	  input: SpineFoldInput,
44	): ContextMessage[] {
45	  const spans = outermostClosedSpans(input.state);
46	  let spanIndex = 0;
47	  let userAnchor = 0;
48	  const out: ContextMessage[] = [];
49	
50	  for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i++) {
51	    const message = messages[i];
52	    if (message === undefined) continue;
53	
54	    if (i < input.state.epochStartAt) {
55	      if (
56	        input.epochSummaryMessage !== undefined &&
57	        input.state.epochMemoryAt !== undefined &&
58	        i === input.state.epochMemoryAt
59	      ) {
60	        out.push(input.epochSummaryMessage);
61	      }
62	      continue;
63	    }
64	
65	    const span = spans[spanIndex];
66	    if (span !== undefined && i === span.openedAt) {
67	      const memoryMessage = spineMemoryMessage(span.node);
68	      if (memoryMessage !== undefined) out.push(memoryMessage);
69	      i = span.closedAt;
70	      spanIndex += 1;
71	      continue;
72	    }
73	
74	    if (isUserRequest(message)) {
75	      userAnchor += 1;
76	      out.push(annotateUserRequest(message, userAnchor));
77	    } else {
78	      out.push(message);
79	    }
80	  }
81	
82	  if (input.status !== undefined) {
83	    out.push(statusMessage(input.status));
84	  }
85	
86	  return out;
87	}
88	
89	function outermostClosedSpans(state: SpineState): readonly ClosedSpan[] {
90	  const closed = new Map<string, SpineNode>();
91	  for (const node of Object.values(state.nodes)) {
92	    if (
93	      node.closedAt !== undefined &&
94	      node.openedAt >= 0 &&
95	      node.memory !== undefined
96	    ) {
97	      closed.set(node.id, node);
98	    }
99	  }
100	
101	  const spans: ClosedSpan[] = [];
102	  for (const node of closed.values()) {
103	    if (!hasClosedAncestor(node, state, closed)) {
104	      spans.push({ node, openedAt: node.openedAt, closedAt: node.closedAt ?? node.openedAt });
105	    }
106	  }
107	  spans.sort((a, b) => a.openedAt - b.openedAt);
108	  return spans;
109	}
110	
111	function hasClosedAncestor(
112	  node: SpineNode,
113	  state: SpineState,
114	  closed: ReadonlyMap<string, SpineNode>,
115	): boolean {
116	  let ancestorId = parentNodeId(node.id);
117	  while (ancestorId !== null) {
118	    if (closed.has(ancestorId)) return true;
119	    const ancestor = state.nodes[ancestorId];
120	    ancestorId = ancestor === undefined ? null : parentNodeId(ancestor.id);
121	  }
122	  return false;
123	}
124	
125	function spineMemoryMessage(node: SpineNode): ContextMessage | undefined {
126	  const memory = node.memory;
127	  if (memory === undefined) return undefined;
128	  return {
129	    role: 'user',
130	    content: [{ type: 'text', text: `<spine_memory>\n${memory}\n</spine_memory>` }],
131	    toolCalls: [],
132	    origin: { kind: 'injection', variant: 'spine_memory' },
133	  };
134	}
135	
136	function isUserRequest(message: ContextMessage): boolean {
137	  return message.role === 'user' && message.origin?.kind === 'user';
138	}
139	
140	function annotateUserRequest(
141	  message: ContextMessage,
142	  anchorNumber: number,
143	): ContextMessage {
144	  const anchor = `[U${String(anchorNumber)}] `;
145	  const content = prefixFirstText(message.content, anchor);
146	  if (content === message.content) return message;
147	  return { ...message, content };
148	}
149	
150	function prefixFirstText(
151	  content: readonly ContentPart[],
152	  anchor: string,
153	): ContentPart[] {
154	  const index = content.findIndex((part) => part.type === 'text');
155	  if (index < 0) {
156	    return [{ type: 'text', text: anchor.trimEnd() }, ...content];
157	  }
158	  return content.map((part, position) =>
159	    position === index && part.type === 'text' ? { type: 'text', text: anchor + part.text } : part,
160	  );
161	}
162	
163	function statusMessage(status: SpineFoldStatus): ContextMessage {
164	  const parent = status.parentId === null ? '' : ` parent="${status.parentId}"`;
165	  const cursorContext = ` cursor_context="~${formatTokens(status.cursorContext)}"`;
166	  const contextLeft =
167	    status.contextLeft === undefined ? '' : ` context_left="~${formatTokens(status.contextLeft)}"`;
168	  const text = `<spine_status cursor="${status.cursorId}" summary="${escapeAttr(status.summary)}"${parent}${cursorContext}${contextLeft} />`;
169	  return {
170	    role: 'user',
171	    content: [{ type: 'text', text }],
172	    toolCalls: [],
173	    origin: { kind: 'injection', variant: 'spine_status' },
174	  };
175	}
176	
177	function formatTokens(tokens: number): string {
178	  const safe = Math.max(0, tokens);
179	  if (safe >= 1000) return `${(safe / 1000).toFixed(safe >= 10000 ? 0 : 1)}K`;
180	  return String(safe);
181	}
182	
183	function escapeAttr(value: string): string {
184	  return value.replaceAll('&', '&amp;').replaceAll('"', '&quot;').replaceAll('<', '&lt;');
185	}

131	  acceptOpen(summary: string, toolCallId: string): SpineTransitionResult {

157	    if (isRootEpoch(this.cursorId())) return REJECT_ROOT_EPOCH;
158	    this.pending = { kind: 'next', toolCallId, summary: trimmedSummary, memory: trimmedMemory };
159	    return { accepted: true };

163	    const state = this.state();

173	    if (!this.enabled) return messages;
174	    const state = this.state();
175	    const epochSummaryMessage =
176	      state.epochMemoryAt === undefined ? undefined : messages[state.epochMemoryAt];

180	  private buildStatus(): SpineFoldStatus {
181	    const state = this.state();
182	    const cursorId = topOf(state);
183	    const cursor = state.nodes[cursorId];
184	    const summary = cursor?.summary ?? '';
185	    const openedAt = cursor === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(0, cursor.openedAt);
186	    const maxContextTokens = this.profile.getModelCapabilities().max_context_tokens;
187	    const used = this.contextSize.get().size;
188	    const contextLeft =
189	      maxContextTokens !== undefined && maxContextTokens > 0
190	        ? Math.max(0, maxContextTokens - used)
191	        : undefined;

196	      cursorContext: this.contextSize.get(openedAt).size,
197	      contextLeft,

235	  private async commitPending(): Promise<void> {
236	    if (!this.enabled) return;
237	    const pending = this.pending;

239	
240	    const history = this.context.get();
241	    const evidence = findEvidence(history, this.lastObservedIndex, pending.toolCallId);
242	    this.lastObservedIndex = history.length;
243	    if (evidence === null) return;
244	
245	    switch (pending.kind) {
246	      case 'open':
247	        this.commitOpen(pending.summary, evidence.assistantIndex);
248	        break;
249	      case 'close':
250	        await this.commitClose(pending.memory, evidence.toolResultIndex);
251	        break;
252	      case 'next':
253	        await this.commitNext(pending.summary, pending.memory, evidence.toolResultIndex);
254	        break;
255	    }
256	    this.pending = null;
257	  }

State shape + apply:

```ts
export type ContextSizeSnapshotKind = 'measured' | 'estimate';

export interface ContextSizeSnapshot {
  readonly storageLength: number;
  readonly tokens: number;
  readonly kind: ContextSizeSnapshotKind;
}

export interface ContextSizeState {
  readonly length: number;
  readonly tokens: number;
  readonly snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[];
}

export const ContextSizeModel = defineModel<ContextSizeState>('contextSize', () => ({
  length: 0, tokens: 0, snapshots: [],
}));

export interface ContextSizeMeasuredPayload {
  readonly length: number;
  readonly tokens: number;
  readonly kind?: ContextSizeSnapshotKind; // default 'measured'
}

const MAX_SNAPSHOTS = 64;

apply: (s, p) => {
  const length = normalizeMeasuredLength(p.length);
  const tokens = Math.max(0, p.tokens);
  const kind = p.kind ?? 'measured';
  const snapshot: ContextSizeSnapshot = { storageLength: length, tokens, kind };
  let snapshots: ContextSizeSnapshot[];
  if (kind === 'estimate') {
    // Content-mutation cascades rebase the prefix: every older snapshot refers
    // to a superseded message layout, so the chain restarts at the estimate.
    snapshots = [snapshot];
  } else {
    snapshots = [...snapshots.filter(snap => snap.storageLength < length), snapshot];
    if (snapshots.length > MAX_SNAPSHOTS) snapshots = snapshots.slice(snapshots.length - MAX_SNAPSHOTS);
  }
  if (s.length === length && s.tokens === tokens && snapshotsEqual(s.snapshots, snapshots)) return s;
  return { length, tokens, snapshots };
},
```

typescript readonly arrays: use immutable ops. snapshotsEqual: same length + element fields ===.

Service get():
```ts
get(start?, end?): ContextSize {
  const context = this.context.get();
  const model = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel);
  const from = normalize(start ?? 0), to = normalize(end ?? context.length);
  const measuredEnd = Math.min(to, model.length);
  const estimatedStart = Math.max(from, model.length);
  const measured =
    from < measuredEnd
      ? (from === 0 && measuredEnd === model.length
        ? model.tokens
        : this.measuredRange(context, model, from, measuredEnd))
      : 0;
  const estimated = estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(estimatedStart, to));
  return { size: measured + estimated, measured, estimated };
}
```

Note: when from >= measuredEnd, old code yields measured = estimate(slice(from,measuredEnd)) = estimate([]) = 0 ✓ same as my explicit 0 — keep old shape actually simpler: measured = (from===0 && measuredEnd===model.length) ? model.tokens : measuredRange(...) where measuredRange(from,measuredEnd) with empty slice → 0 naturally (P both sides... from==to: pFrom==pTo → d=0 or est[]=0 ✓). ```

measuredSubRange:
```ts
function measuredSubRange(
  context: readonly ContextMessage[],
  snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[],
  from: number,
  to: number,
): number {
  const prefixFrom = prefixTokens(context, snapshots, from);
  const prefixTo = prefixTokens(context, snapshots, to);
  if (prefixFrom === undefined || prefixTo === undefined) {
    return estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(from, to));
  }
  const diff = prefixTo - prefixFrom;
  if (diff < 0) {
    // Snapshot anchors disagree with the live estimate strips (chain crossed a
    // content mutation) — trust the plain estimate instead.
    return estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(from, to));
  }
  return diff;
}

/** Tokens of the storage prefix [0, end): latest snapshot at or before `end`
 * plus a narrow per-message estimate strip past it; undefined when no snapshot
 * reaches `end`. */
function prefixTokens(
  context: readonly ContextMessage[],
  snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[],
  end: number,
): number | undefined {
  let anchor: ContextSizeSnapshot | undefined;
  for (const snap of snapshots) {
    if (snap.storageLength <= end) anchor = snap;
  }
  if (anchor === undefined) return undefined;
  return anchor.tokens + estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(anchor.storageLength, end));
}
```

latestMeasurement():
```ts
latestMeasurement(): ContextSizeMeasurement | undefined {
  const snapshots = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel).snapshots;
  const latest = snapshots.at(-1);
  if (latest === undefined) return undefined;
  return { length: latest.storageLength, tokens: latest.tokens, kind: latest.kind };
}
```

tool_call: Write({"path":"packages/agent-core-v2/src/agent/contextSize/contextSizeOps.ts","content":"/**\n * `contextSize` domain (L4) — wire Model (`ContextSizeModel`) and the\n * `context_size.measured` (`contextSizeMeasured`) Op for the last measured\n * context token count.\n *\n * Declares the deterministic measured prefix as `{ length, tokens }` (initial\n * `{ 0, 0 }`): the length (in messages) and total token count of the most\n * recent `context_size.measured` record. That record is written from two live\n * paths: `llmRequester` after each measured exchange (a true LLM-reported\n * count), and `contextMemoryService` / `fullCompactionService` cascading\n * alongside every context mutation that changes the measured prefix (`clear`\n * resets, `applyCompaction` adopts `tokensAfter`, `undo`/`sizeOpsForCut`\n * rebases to an estimate, epoch compaction adopts the post-compaction total);\n * `append` is intentionally not cascaded because new messages are the\n * unmeasured tail.\n *\n * `snapshots` is a live-only chain of those records (`storageLength` = prefix\n * length at write time, `tokens` = prefix total, `kind`): it lets\n * `contextSizeService.get()` size a measured sub-range by DIFFERENCING two\n * records instead of re-estimating message text (e.g. the spine cursor-context\n * between the request measured at node-open and the latest one). Convergence\n * rules: an estimate-kind record is written by content mutations whose new\n * prefix describes different message text, so it RESTARTS the chain (older\n * snapshots refer to a superseded layout); a measured-kind record drops\n * snapshots at or beyond its own length, replaces a same-length snapshot, and\n * keeps the chain FIFO-bounded at 64 so only recent exchange pairs diff.\n * Token totals are request-caliber (provider usage counts system prompt,\n * tools, and the spine status line too), so a diff between records also\n * absorbs any system-prompt/tool drift between the two exchanges — accepted\n * status-line noise. The Op is live-only because `context_size.measured` is\n * not a v1 record type: resume starts from `{ 0, 0, [] }` and\n * `contextSizeService.get()` estimates until the next measured exchange.\n * `apply` is pure — it normalizes the payload and returns the SAME reference\n * on a no-op so the wire's reference-equality gate stays quiet — and carries\n * no non-determinism (the last measured record wins). The sparse\n * `measuredPrefixTokens` array and the per-message live `estimates` are\n * intentionally NOT in the Model. Consumed by the Agent-scope\n * `contextSizeService`.\n */\n\nimport { defineModel } from '#/wire/model';\nimport { defineOp } from '#/wire/op';\n\nexport type ContextSizeSnapshotKind = 'measured' | 'estimate';\n\nexport interface ContextSizeSnapshot {\n  readonly storageLength: number;\n  readonly tokens: number;\n  readonly kind: ContextSizeSnapshotKind;\n}\n\nexport interface ContextSizeState {\n  readonly length: number;\n  readonly tokens: number;\n  readonly snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[];\n}\n\nconst MAX_SNAPSHOTS = 64;\n\nexport const ContextSizeModel = defineModel<ContextSizeState>('contextSize', () => ({\n  length: 0,\n  tokens: 0,\n  snapshots: [],\n}));\n\nexport interface ContextSizeMeasuredPayload {\n  readonly length: number;\n  readonly tokens: number;\n  /**\n   * Provenance of the record. `measured` (default) = LLM-reported usage;\n   * `estimate` = cascade computed alongside a content mutation (clear,\n   * compaction, undo rebase) — restarts the snapshot chain.\n   */\n  readonly kind?: ContextSizeSnapshotKind;\n}\n\nexport const contextSizeMeasured = defineOp(ContextSizeModel, 'context_size.measured', {\n  persist: false,\n  apply: (s, p: ContextSizeMeasuredPayload): ContextSizeState => {\n    const length = normalizeMeasuredLength(p.length);\n    const tokens = Math.max(0, p.tokens);\n    const kind = p.kind ?? 'measured';\n    const snapshot: ContextSizeSnapshot = { storageLength: length, tokens, kind };\n    let snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[];\n    if (kind === 'estimate') {\n      // Content mutations rebase or replace message text: older snapshots\n      // describe a superseded layout, so the chain restarts here.\n      snapshots = [snapshot];\n    } else {\n      snapshots = [...s.snapshots.filter((snap) => snap.storageLength < length), snapshot];\n      if (snapshots.length > MAX_SNAPSHOTS) {\n        snapshots = snapshots.slice(snapshots.length - MAX_SNAPSHOTS);\n      }\n    }\n    if (\n      s.length === length &&\n      s.tokens === tokens &&\n      sameSnapshots(s.snapshots, snapshots)\n    ) {\n      return s;\n    }\n    return { length, tokens, snapshots };\n  },\n  toEvent: (_p, state) => ({\n    type: 'agent.status.updated' as const,\n    contextTokens: state.tokens,\n  }),\n});\n\nfunction sameSnapshots(\n  a: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[],\n  b: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[],\n): boolean {\n  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;\n  for (let index = 0; index < a.length; index += 1) {\n    const left = a[index];\n    const right = b[index];\n    if (\n      left === undefined ||\n      right === undefined ||\n      left.storageLength !== right.storageLength ||\n      left.tokens !== right.tokens ||\n      left.kind !== right.kind\n    ) {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction normalizeMeasuredLength(length: number): number {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(length)) return 0;\n  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(length));\n}\n"}) [id=Write_82]

tool_call: Write({"path":"packages/agent-core-v2/src/agent/contextSize/contextSizeService.ts","content":"/**\n * `contextSize` domain (L4) — `IAgentContextSizeService` implementation.\n *\n * Owns the measured context token counts in the wire `ContextSizeModel`:\n * reads it through `wire.getModel`, writes it through\n * `wire.dispatch(contextSizeMeasured(...))` (called by `llmRequester` after\n * each measured exchange), and emits the `contextTokens` slice of\n * `agent.status.updated` live through `wire.signal` when the measured value\n * changes. `get(start?, end?)` returns `{ size, measured, estimated }` for the\n * context-message range `[start, end)`, resolved like `Array.prototype.slice`\n * (defaulting to the whole context; negative indices count back from the end;\n * an inverted range is empty). `measured` resolves as follows: the full\n * measured-prefix aggregate is exact; a measured-prefix SUB-range diffs the\n * nearest snapshot at or before each endpoint (plus a narrow estimate strip\n * past each anchor) when both endpoints anchor, and otherwise falls back to a\n * per-message estimate; `estimated` is the live token estimate of the\n * not-yet-measured portion, and `size = measured + estimated`. Snapshot-chain\n * bookkeeping lives in `contextSizeOps` (notably: cascade estimate records\n * restart the chain, since their new prefix describes replaced message text).\n * Bound at Agent scope.\n */\n\nimport { Disposable } from '#/_base/di/lifecycle';\nimport { InstantiationType } from '#/_base/di/extensions';\nimport { LifecycleScope, registerScopedService } from '#/_base/di/scope';\nimport { estimateTokensForMessages } from '#/_base/utils/tokens';\nimport { IAgentContextMemoryService } from '#/agent/contextMemory/contextMemory';\nimport type { ContextMessage } from '#/agent/contextMemory/types';\nimport type { Message } from '#/app/llmProtocol/message';\nimport type { TokenUsage } from '#/app/llmProtocol/usage';\nimport { IAgentWireService } from '#/wire/tokens';\nimport type { IWireService } from '#/wire/wireService';\n\nimport { IAgentContextSizeService, type ContextSize, type ContextSizeMeasurement } from './contextSize';\nimport { ContextSizeModel, type ContextSizeSnapshot, contextSizeMeasured } from './contextSizeOps';\n\nexport class AgentContextSizeService extends Disposable implements IAgentContextSizeService {\n  declare readonly _serviceBrand: undefined;\n\n  private lastEmittedTokens = 0;\n\n  constructor(\n    @IAgentContextMemoryService private readonly context: IAgentContextMemoryService,\n    @IAgentWireService private readonly wire: IWireService,\n  ) {\n    super();\n  }\n\n  get(start?: number, end?: number): ContextSize {\n    const context = this.context.get();\n    const model = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel);\n    // Mirrors `Array.prototype.slice`: defaults to the whole context, negative\n    // indices count back from the end, and an inverted range is empty.\n    const from = normalizeSliceIndex(start ?? 0, context.length);\n    const to = normalizeSliceIndex(end ?? context.length, context.length);\n    const measuredEnd = Math.min(to, model.length);\n    const estimatedStart = Math.max(from, model.length);\n    // The measured-prefix total is the only deterministic measured value; use\n    // it when the range covers the whole prefix.\n    const measured =\n      from === 0 && measuredEnd === model.length\n        ? model.tokens\n        : measuredSubRange(context, model.snapshots, from, measuredEnd);\n    const estimated = estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(estimatedStart, to));\n    return { size: measured + estimated, measured, estimated };\n  }\n\n  measured(input: readonly Message[], output: readonly Message[], usage: TokenUsage): void {\n    // Only adopt the measurement when `input` still matches the live context.\n    // This rejects stale readings (e.g. the context was spliced, or the request\n    // used overridden messages) so a mismatched measurement cannot poison state.\n    if (!matchesContext(input, this.context.get())) return;\n    const length = input.length + output.length;\n    const tokens = tokenUsageTotal(usage);\n    this.wire.dispatch(contextSizeMeasured({ length, tokens }));\n    this.emitIfChanged();\n  }\n\n  latestMeasurement(): ContextSizeMeasurement | undefined {\n    const snapshots = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel).snapshots;\n    const latest = snapshots.at(-1);\n    if (latest === undefined) return undefined;\n    return { length: latest.storageLength, tokens: latest.tokens, kind: latest.kind };\n  }\n\n  private emitIfChanged(): void {\n    const tokens = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel).tokens;\n    if (tokens === this.lastEmittedTokens) return;\n    this.lastEmittedTokens = tokens;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Measured size of the stored range `[from, to)`. Diffs snapshot anchors when\n * both endpoints resolve one; a range that can't anchor (no snapshot reaches\n * the endpoint, or the diff came out negative because anchors straddle a\n * mutation) falls back to the per-message estimate.\n */\nfunction measuredSubRange(\n  context: readonly ContextMessage[],\n  snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[],\n  from: number,\n  to: number,\n): number {\n  const prefixFrom = prefixTokens(context, snapshots, from);\n  const prefixTo = prefixTokens(context, snapshots, to);\n  if (prefixFrom === undefined || prefixTo === undefined || prefixTo < prefixFrom) {\n    return estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(from, to));\n  }\n  return prefixTo - prefixFrom;\n}\n\n/**\n * Tokens of the storage prefix `[0, end)`: the newest snapshot at or before\n * `end`, plus a narrow per-message estimate of the strip past it. `undefined`\n * when no snapshot reaches `end`.\n */\nfunction prefixTokens(\n  context: readonly ContextMessage[],\n  snapshots: readonly ContextSizeSnapshot[],\n  end: number,\n): number | undefined {\n  let anchor: ContextSizeSnapshot | undefined;\n  for (const snapshot of snapshots) {\n    if (snapshot.storageLength <= end) anchor = snapshot;\n  }\n  if (anchor === undefined) return undefined;\n  return anchor.tokens + estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(anchor.storageLength, end));\n}\n\nfunction matchesContext(input: readonly Message[], context: readonly ContextMessage[]): boolean {\n  if (input.length !== context.length) return false;\n  for (let index = 0; index < input.length; index += 1) {\n    if (input[index] !== context[index]) return false;\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction tokenUsageTotal(usage: TokenUsage): number {\n  return usage.inputCacheRead + usage.inputCacheCreation + usage.inputOther + usage.output;\n}\n\nfunction normalizeSliceIndex(index: number, length: number): number {\n  if (index < 0) return Math.max(length + index, 0);\n  return Math.min(index, length);\n}\n\nregisterScopedService(\n  LifecycleScope.Agent,\n  IAgentContextSizeService,\n  AgentContextSizeService,\n  InstantiationType.Delayed,\n  'contextSize',\n);\n"}) [id=Write_84]

The subagent runs as a same-process loop instance with its own context and wire file. Delegating also keeps the bulk of intermediate file contents out of your own context — you get a conclusion back instead of a pile of dumps.\n\nWriting the prompt:\n- The subagent starts with zero context — it has not seen this conversation. Brief it like a colleague who just walked into the room: state the goal, list what you already know, hand over the specifics.\n- Lookups (read this file, run that test): put the exact path or command in the prompt. The subagent should not have to search for things you already know.\n- Investigations (figure out X, find why Y): give the question, not prescribed steps — fixed steps become dead weight when the premise is wrong.\n- Do not delegate understanding. If the task hinges on a file path or line number, find it yourself first and write it into the prompt.\n\nUsage notes:\n- When the task continues earlier work a subagent already did, prefer resuming that agent (pass its `resume` id) over spawning a fresh instance — the resumed agent keeps its prior context.\n- A subagent's result is only visible to you, not to the user. When the user needs to see what a subagent produced, summarize the relevant parts yourself in your own reply.\n- Subagents use a fixed 30-minute timeout. If one times out, resume the same agent instead of starting over.\n\nWhen NOT to use Agent: skip delegation for trivial work you can do directly — reading a file whose path you already know, searching a small known set of files, or any task that takes only a step or two. Delegation has a context-handoff cost; it pays off only when the task is substantial enough to outweigh it.\n\nOnce a subagent is running, leave that scope to it: do not redo its searches or reads i[...truncated]

    }

  private commitOpen(summary: string, openedAt: number): void {
    const state = this.state();
    const parentId = topOf(state);
    const parent = state.nodes[parentId];
    if (parent === undefined) return;
    const id = childNodeId(parentId, nextChildIndex(parent.children));
    this.wire.dispatch(
      spineOpen({
        id,
        summary,
        parentId,
        openedAt,
        baselineTokens: this.contextSize.get().size,
      }),
    );
  }

  private async commitClose(memory: string, closedAt: number): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.state();
    const id = topOf(state);
    const node = state.nodes[id];
    if (node === undefined || isRootEpoch(id)) return;
    const assembled = assembleMemoryBody({
      childMemories: closedChildMemories(state, node),
      nodeMemory: memory,
    });
    const closing: SpineNode = { ...node, closedAt, memory: assembled };
    const archivePath = await this.archiveNode(closing);
    this.wire.dispatch(spineClose({ id, closedAt, memory: assembled, archivePath }));
  }

  private async commitNext(summary: string, memory: string, closedAt: number): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.state();
    const closedId = topOf(state);
    const closing = state.nodes[closedId];
    if (closing === undefined || isRootEpoch(closedId)) return;
    const parentId = parentNodeId(closedId);
    if (parentId === null) return;
